# kgc-bench — results

Generated by `python -m harness.report` from `conformance.csv`, `perf.csv` and `machine.json`; do not edit by hand.

## RQ1 — Conformance to the RML test suites

Each case is solved twice by a SPARQL-Generate engine: by a hand-written query (`conformance/hand/`, mode `hand`), and by the query that `rml2sparql-generate` generates from the case's mapping (mode `translated`); an RML engine runs the mapping itself (mode `rml`), with its default options and N-Quads output, in a directory that holds the mapping and the case's input documents. A case passes when the output is isomorphic to the expected output (RDF dataset isomorphism, named graphs and RDF-star quoted triples included) and every expected target file (RML-IO) is written and isomorphic, or, for a case that expects an error, when an error is raised: a non-zero exit code, a timeout, the memory cap, or an error in the log, the same rule for every engine. A feature that the engine or the translator does not support is a failure; an engine that refuses it with an error does pass the cases of that feature that expect an error (in rml-lv, 10 of the 41 cases expect one). N/A (mode `hand` only): an invalid mapping has no hand-written counterpart, and the module suites (rml-io, rml-fnml, rml-cc, rml-star, rml-lv) have no hand-written queries. Graph maps are translated into `GRAPH` blocks of the `GENERATE` template (SPARQL-Generate 2.4.0 and later), whose output, in N-Quads, is compared graph by graph.

## RQ3 — Performance

**Limits.** Every run of every engine has the same limits: 24 GB of memory and 10 min of wall time. A run over a limit is stopped: the solution is *not viable* at that size, and is neither replayed nor tried at a larger size. Runs measured before the limits were set are classified the same way, from their peak memory and time.

**Provisional timings.** Measured on FAYOL-IOT-SERVEUR (Intel(R) Xeon(R) E-2146G CPU @ 3.50GHz, 12 CPUs, 31.2 GB RAM, Debian GNU/Linux 13 (trixie); openjdk version "21.0.13.1" 2026-11-17; Python 3.13.5) on 2026-11-26T23:31:33, while other jobs were running (load average 0.68/1.20/1.31 before, 1.30/1.37/1.40 after): to be re-measured on a quiet machine.

Cold processes, best of the runs (wall time, start-up included) and the peak resident memory of that run; distinct triples of the output. ✓: the output is the reference output (Morph-KGC) at the smallest scale; =: same number of distinct triples as the reference at a larger scale; ✗: it differs (see the notes). Java engines: same JDK, same maximum heap.

## Positioning — semantics drift under slicing

The same questions (`positioning/slicing/queries/`) over 24 people in CSV, JSON and XML. SPARQL Anything: with slicing, and batches of 2, 10 and 1000 slices, against `slice=false`; SPARQL-Generate: the iterator in batches of 1, 2, 10 and 1000 rows, against one batch. A question changes when its multiset of solutions differs from the reference's (`python -m harness.slicing`).

## Positioning — feature matrix

Minimal executable cases (`positioning/features/cases.py`), each with its input and expected output (`python -m harness.features`): pass, fail, error, not expressible (the engine has no construct for it), not run (needs a service this machine does not have).

| case | SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release) | SPARQL Anything 1.3.0 |
|---|---|---|
| rdf-list (an ordered rdf:List of the values of a JSON array) | pass | pass |
| html-text (an HTML list (text, not RDF) from a CSV file) | pass | pass |
| tree (the parent-child pairs of a JSON tree of unknown depth) | pass | pass |
| join-csv-json (a join of a CSV file and a JSON file on a key) | pass | pass |
| bnode-per-value (one blank node per city, shared by the rows of that city) | pass | pass |
| json-lines (JSON Lines: one JSON object per line) | pass | fail |
| yaml (a YAML document) | not expressible | pass |
| xlsx (the rows of a spreadsheet) | not expressible | pass |
| markdown-headings (the headings of a Markdown file) | pass | pass |
| cbor (a CBOR document) | fail | fail |
| cbor-base64 (the same CBOR document, given in base64 in a text file) | fail | not expressible |
| named-graphs (each person in the named graph of their city (quads)) | pass | pass |
| http-header (one HTTP GET per CSV row, the body and a response header in the output) | pass | fail |
| html-css (the elements of an HTML page selected by a CSS selector) | pass | pass |
| mqtt (the messages of an MQTT topic for 60 s) | not run | not expressible |
| sql-table (the rows of a relational table) | not run | not expressible |
| exif (the EXIF metadata of an image) | not expressible | not run |

- json-lines, SPARQL Anything 1.3.0: [main] WARN io.github.sparqlanything.engine.FXGraphMaterialisationStrategy - No triplifier found; different named graphs: got [], expected ['None']
- yaml, SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release): no YAML reader (sources: JSON, CSV, XML, HTML, CBOR, GeoJSON, SQL, text)
- xlsx, SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release): no spreadsheet reader
- cbor, SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release): SOURCE reads a file as a string, and iter:CBOR wants the document in base64: a binary file cannot be read (see cbor-base64); e.ci.sparql_generate.engine.IteratorPlan:155 - Unanticipated exception for ITERATOR http://w3id
- cbor, SPARQL Anything 1.3.0: [main] WARN io.github.sparqlanything.engine.FXGraphMaterialisationStrategy - No triplifier found; different named graphs: got [], expected ['None']
- cbor-base64, SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release): iter:CBOR decodes the base64, then parses the bytes as JSON (InvalidJsonException, logged at DEBUG only): a defect of 2.1.0 to 2.2.3; different named graphs: got [], expected ['None']
- cbor-base64, SPARQL Anything 1.3.0: no CBOR triplifier (see cbor)
- named-graphs, SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release): GENERATE writes triples (quad templates are planned for 2.3): a TEMPLATE writes N-Quads
- http-header, SPARQL Anything 1.3.0: the facade holds the body of a response, not its headers; graph (default) differs (2 vs 4 triples, 0 and 2 apart); extra: -; missing: <http://example.org/item/1> <http://example.org/version> "7"
- mqtt, SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release): iter:MQTTSubscribe; needs an MQTT broker, and none runs on this machine
- mqtt, SPARQL Anything 1.3.0: no stream source
- sql-table, SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release): iter:SQL (JDBC; the MySQL driver is in the JAR); needs a database server, and none runs on this machine
- sql-table, SPARQL Anything 1.3.0: no relational source
- exif, SPARQL-Generate 2.2.3 (release): no image metadata reader
- exif, SPARQL Anything 1.3.0: the Metadata triplifier; needs an image with EXIF metadata (no image library in this environment)
